- **jos-author-response**: 当你收到《软件学报》(Journal of Software, JOS) 的外审意见、需要撰写修回说明（答复审稿人）并修改稿件时使用。覆盖逐条回应的结构、"必须改/可讨论/礼貌不改"的分级策略、修回说明与稿件修改处的对应标注、补做实验的取舍、多轮修回之间的一致性维护、以及与责任编委和主编终审衔接的中文表达分寸，帮助你把《软件学报》(Journal of Software) 的"修改后再审"稳妥地做成录用，而不是在下一轮被退稿。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Software-Skills/skills/jos-author-response) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

- **jos-experiments**: 当你要为投向《软件学报》(Journal of Software, JOS) 的稿件设计或打磨实验与评测时使用。覆盖研究问题 (RQ) 契约、真实系统与数据集的选取、可信基线、评价指标与统计显著性/效应量、消融与抗污染的大模型评测、挖掘类研究的出处锁定、以及威胁有效性 (threats to validity) 的分类论证，帮助你把证据与论点对齐，达到《软件学报》(Journal of Software) 软件学科同行评审对实证严谨性的要求。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-Software-Skills/skills/jos-experiments) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

It never files a draft. ([Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-](https://github.com/Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/josh-hook-lab))
- **josh-infographic**: Use when a post's content has structure worth showing — ranked methods, a comparison, a sequence of steps, a set of numbers measured across a population. Builds it as a self-contained SVG in his brand and files it with create_visual. Use josh-visual for a single object or a single number; use this when the shape of the information is the point. ([Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-](https://github.com/Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/josh-infographic))
- **josh-kushner**: Use Josh Kushner's documented source notes when the user asks to summon Josh Kushner or uses /josh-kushner. Fetch public retrieval without login or MCP; keep personal context in this chat. ([adamtpang/summon.guide](https://github.com/adamtpang/summon.guide/tree/HEAD/packs/handles/josh-kushner))
- **josh-long-coding-style**: Josh Long的Spring Boot编码风格蒸馏 - 生产级Spring最佳实践 ([wjy0705/java-coding-distill](https://github.com/wjy0705/java-coding-distill/tree/HEAD/josh-long-coding-style))
- **josh-quote-card**: Use when a post has one line worth lifting out — a sentence someone said to him, the line the post turns on, a number stated plainly. Sets it in DM Serif Display on his cream ground as a single card. The cheapest image in the set and the one that travels when someone reshares. ([Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-](https://github.com/Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/josh-quote-card))
- **josh-visual**: Use when a post needs a picture — "make an image for this", "what goes with this post", a draft Josh is about to approve that would carry better with a visual, or a rebuild of something he has seen and shared. Writes a self-contained SVG in Slingshot GTM's brand and files it with create_visual so it attaches to the post. Fetches the brand from the library rather than from any palette written down here. ([Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-](https://github.com/Sai-Prashanth123/voice-to-LinkedIn-/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/josh-visual))
